  320: 
  321: func TestResponseAPIUsageToModelMatchesRealLog(t *testing.T) {
  322: 	payload := []byte(`{"input_tokens":8555,"input_tokens_details":{"cached_tokens":4224},"output_tokens":889,"output_tokens_details":{"reasoning_tokens":640},"total_tokens":9444}`)
